Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The parabolic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 937 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 214 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 937 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the parabolic structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 214. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
